# P3-10 Compatibility and Execution Matrix

Generated from frozen reports and manifests by `scripts/phase3-p310-gate-audit.py`; statuses are terminal accounting, not a support declaration.

- Frozen catalog: 5690 entries / 11380 entry×OS positions.
- P3-05 affected units: Linux 2206 + Windows 973 = 3179 unique units.
- P3-05 effective unit outcomes (passed / failed / skipped / unknown): 1299 / 688 / 1139 / 53.
- P3-08 behavior ledger: 120 positions / 360 attempt slots; dispatched 0; behavior gate `not_evaluated`.
- No catalog-wide compatibility or support claim is made. Windows Sandbox evidence is Evaluation/Audit Mode.

## P3-10 Gate Rulings

| Gate | Ruling | Scope and unresolved boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P3-ENGINE | `passed_scoped` | Windows catalog_plugin_claims_allowed=false |
| P3-RECOVERY | `blocked` | 53 terminal unknown outcomes remain: Linux 39, Windows 14. |
| P3-AUTOMATION | `blocked` | The same 53 P3-05 unknowns block automation health. |
| P3-BEHAVIOR | `not_evaluated` | 0 attempts dispatched; all 120 positions lack executable Host event/setup bindings. |
| P3-FIELD | `not_run` | No real-platform acceptance was included in this plan. |
